PERCENTILE.INC.\n\n    Algorithm:\n      - Sort ascending.\n      - pos = (n-1)*k + 1 (1-indexed)\n      - If pos integer => that value\n      - Else interpolate between floor and ceil ranks.\n    \"\"\"\n    vals = [v for v in values if v is not None]\n    if not vals:\n        return None\n    if k < 0 or k > 1:\n        raise ValueError('k must be between 0 and 1')\n    vals = sorted(vals)\n    n = len(vals)\n    if n == 1:\n        return vals[0]\n\n    pos = (n - 1) * k + 1\n    if abs(pos - round(pos)) < 1e-12:\n        idx = int(round(pos)) - 1\n        return vals[idx]\n\n    lo_rank = int(math.floor(pos))\n    hi_rank = int(math.ceil(pos))\n    lo = vals[lo_rank - 1]\n    hi = vals[hi_rank - 1]\n    frac = pos - lo_rank\n    return lo + (hi - lo) * frac\n\n\ndef get_task_sheet_xml_path(zf: zipfile.ZipFile, sheet_name: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Return 'xl/worksheets/sheetN.xml' for a given sheet name.\"\"\"\n    wb_xml = ET.fromstring(zf.read('xl/workbook.xml'))\n    ns = {'ns': 'http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/main'}\n    sheets_el = wb_xml.find('ns:sheets', ns)\n    if sheets_el is None:\n        raise RuntimeError('workbook.xml missing <sheets>')\n\n    target_rid = None\n    for sh in sheets_el.findall('ns:sheet', ns):\n        if sh.attrib.get('name') == sheet_name:\n            target_rid = sh.attrib.get('{http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships}id')\n            break\n    if not target_rid:\n        raise KeyError(f\"Sheet {sheet_name!r} not found\")\n\n    rels_xml = ET.fromstring(zf.read('xl/_rels/workbook.xml.rels'))\n    rels_ns = {'r': 'http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships'}\n    target = None\n    for rel in rels_xml.findall('r:Relationship', rels_ns):\n        if rel.attrib.get('Id') == target_rid:\n            target = rel.attrib.get('Target')\n            break\n    if not target:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"Relationship {target_rid} not found\")\n\n    # Targets are relative to xl/ in most workbooks\n    if target.startswith('/'):\n        # Shouldn't happen for standard xlsx\n        target = target.lstrip('/')\n        if not target.startswith('xl/'):\n            target = 'xl/' + target\n        return target\n\n    # e.g. 'worksheets/sheet1.xml'\n    return 'xl/' + target\n\n\ndef inject_cached_values_into_sheet_xml(sheet_xml_bytes: bytes, values_by_cellref: dict[str, float | int | None]) -> bytes:\n    \"\"\"Given a worksheet XML, ensure each formula cell has a cached <v> numeric value.\"\"\"\n    root = ET.fromstring(sheet_xml_bytes)\n    ns_uri = 'http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/main'\n    ns = {'ns': ns_uri}\n\n    # Map cell reference -> element for quick updates\n    # <worksheet><sheetData><row><c r=\"H12\">...</c>\n    for c_el in root.findall('.//ns:c', ns):\n        r = c_el.attrib.get('r')\n        if not r or r not in values_by_cellref:\n            continue\n\n        val = values_by_cellref[r]\n        if val is None or (isinstance(val, float) and (math.isnan(val) or math.isinf(val))):\n            # Leave as-is; Excel will show error or blank. But we still want something.\n            # Use blank numeric cached value.\n            val_text = ''\n        else:\n            # Keep full precision but avoid scientific where possible.\n            if isinstance(val, bool):\n                val_text = '1' if val else '0'\n            elif isinstance(val, int):\n                val_text = str(val)\n            else:\n                val_text = repr(float(val))\n\n        # Remove type attribute that would force string\n        if c_el.attrib.get('t') == 's':\n            del c_el.attrib['t']\n        if c_el.attrib.get('t') == 'str':\n            del c_el.attrib['t']\n\n        v_el = c_el.find('ns:v', ns)\n        if v_el is None:\n            v_el = ET.SubElement(c_el, f'{{{ns_uri}}}v')\n        v_el.text = val_text\n\n    # Preserve namespaces\n    return ET.tostring(root, encoding='utf-8', xml_declaration=True)\n\n\n# -----------------\n# Load workbook and inspect relevant ranges\n# -----------------\nwb = openpyxl.load_workbook(INPUT_PATH, data_only=False)\nws_task = wb['Task']\nws_data = wb['Data']\n\n# Data header positions discovered from inspection\nDATA_CODE_COL = 4  # D\nDATA_FIRST_ROW = 21\nDATA_LAST_ROW = 38\nDATA_YEAR_HEADER_ROW = 4\nDATA_YEAR_FIRST_COL = 8  # H\nDATA_YEAR_LAST_COL = 12  # L\n\n# Build series_code -> {year -> value}\nyears = []\nfor c in range(DATA_YEAR_FIRST_COL, DATA_YEAR_LAST_COL + 1):\n    years.append(ws_data.cell(DATA_YEAR_HEADER_ROW, c).value)\n\nseries_map: dict[str, dict[int, float]] = {}\nfor r in range(DATA_FIRST_ROW, DATA_LAST_ROW + 1):\n    code = ws_data.cell(r, DATA_CODE_COL).value\n    if not code:\n        continue\n    d = {}\n    for c, yr in zip(range(DATA_YEAR_FIRST_COL, DATA_YEAR_LAST_COL + 1), years):\n        d[int(yr)] = ws_data.cell(r, c).value\n    series_map[str(code)] = d\n\n# Task year headers (H10:L10)\ntask_years = [ws_task.cell(10, c).value for c in range(8, 13)]\n\n# Targets\nLOOKUP_BLOCKS = [\n    (12, 17),\n    (19, 24),\n    (26, 31),\n]\nYEAR_COLS = list(range(8, 13))  # H-L\n\n# We'll compute all numeric results and keep formulas + cached values mapping\ncached_values: dict[str, float | int | None] = {}\n\n# -----------------\n# Step 1: Lookup formulas for the three 6x5 blocks\n# -----------------\nfor r1, r2 in LOOKUP_BLOCKS:\n    for r in range(r1, r2 + 1):\n        series_code = ws_task.cell(r, 4).value  # D\n        if not series_code:\n            continue\n        series_code = str(series_code)\n        for c in YEAR_COLS:\n            year = int(ws_task.cell(10, c).value)\n            col_letter = get_column_letter(c)\n            addr = f\"{col_letter}{r}\"\n            # Formula: INDEX(Data!$H$21:$L$38, MATCH($D{r}, Data!$D$21:$D$38,0), MATCH(H$10, Data!$H$4:$L$4,0))\n            formula = (\n                f\"=INDEX(Data!$H$21:$L$38, \"\n                f\"MATCH($D{r}, Data!$D$21:$D$38, 0), \"\n                f\"MATCH({col_letter}$10, Data!$H$4:$L$4, 0))\"\n            )\n            ws_task[addr].value = formula\n\n            # Compute cached numeric value\n            v = series_map.get(series_code, {}).get(year)\n            cached_values[addr] = v\n\n# -----------------\n# Step 2: Net SLA buffer formulas H35:L40\n# Net SLA buffer = (Preserved - Consumed) / Capacity * 100\n# Mapping by service order:\n#   preserved rows 12-17\n#   consumed   rows 19-24\n#   capacity   rows 26-31\n#   net rows   35-40 correspond to preserved rows 12-17\n# -----------------\nfor i, net_row in enumerate(range(35, 41)):\n    pres_row = 12 + i\n    use_row = 19 + i\n    cap_row = 26 + i\n    for c in YEAR_COLS:\n        col_letter = get_column_letter(c)\n        addr = f\"{col_letter}{net_row}\"\n        formula = f\"=({col_letter}{pres_row}-{col_letter}{use_row})/{col_letter}{cap_row}*100\"\n        ws_task[addr].value = formula\n\n        # Compute cached numeric value\n        year = int(ws_task.cell(10, c).value)\n        pres_code = str(ws_task.cell(pres_row, 4).value)\n        use_code = str(ws_task.cell(use_row, 4).value)\n        cap_code = str(ws_task.cell(cap_row, 4).value)\n        pres = series_map[pres_code][year]\n        use = series_map[use_code][year]\n        cap = series_map[cap_code][year]\n        cached_values[addr] = (pres - use) / cap * 100 if cap not in (None, 0) else None\n\n# -----------------\n# Step 3: Summary statistics H42:L47 (labels are in E42:E47)\n# -----------------\nstat_rows = list(range(42, 48))\nlabels = {r: ws_task.cell(r, 5).value for r in stat_rows}  # column E\n\nfor c in YEAR_COLS:\n    col_letter = get_column_letter(c)\n    net_vals = [cached_values[f\"{col_letter}{r}\"] for r in range(35, 41)]\n    # Filter None\n    vals = [v for v in net_vals if v is not None]\n\n    for r in stat_rows:\n        label = (labels[r] or '').strip() if isinstance(labels[r], str) else labels[r]\n        addr = f\"{col_letter}{r}\"\n        rng = f\"{col_letter}35:{col_letter}40\"\n\n        if label == 'Minimum':\n            ws_task[addr].value = f\"=MIN({rng})\"\n            cached_values[addr] = min(vals) if vals else None\n        elif label == 'Maximum':\n            ws_task[addr].value = f\"=MAX({rng})\"\n            cached_values[addr] = max(vals) if vals else None\n        elif label == 'Median':\n            ws_task[addr].value = f\"=MEDIAN({rng})\"\n            cached_values[addr] = statistics.median(vals) if vals else None\n        elif label == 'Simple mean':\n            ws_task[addr].value = f\"=AVERAGE({rng})\"\n            cached_values[addr] = (sum(vals) / len(vals)) if vals else None\n        elif label == '25th percentile':\n            ws_task[addr].value = f\"=PERCENTILE({rng},0.25)\"\n            cached_values[addr] = excel_percentile_inc(vals, 0.25) if vals else None\n        elif label == '75th percentile':\n            ws_task[addr].value = f\"=PERCENTILE({rng},0.75)\"\n            cached_values[addr] = excel_percentile_inc(vals, 0.75) if vals else None\n        else:\n            raise RuntimeError(f\"Unexpected stat label at E{r}: {labels[r]!r}\")\n\n# -----------------\n# Step 4: Weighted mean H50:L50\n# =SUMPRODUCT(NetBuffer, RequestCap) / SUM(RequestCap)\n# Net: rows 35-40, weights: rows 26-31\n# -----------------\nfor c in YEAR_COLS:\n    col_letter = get_column_letter(c)\n    addr = f\"{col_letter}50\"\n    net_rng = f\"{col_letter}35:{col_letter}40\"\n    w_rng = f\"{col_letter}26:{col_letter}31\"\n    ws_task[addr].value = f\"=SUMPRODUCT({net_rng},{w_rng})/SUM({w_rng})\"\n\n    # Compute cached\n    net_vals = [cached_values[f\"{col_letter}{r}\"] for r in range(35, 41)]\n    year = int(ws_task.cell(10, c).value)\n    weights = []\n    for rr in range(26, 32):\n        cap_code = str(ws_task.cell(rr, 4).value)\n        weights.append(series_map[cap_code][year])\n\n    pairs = [(nv, w) for nv, w in zip(net_vals, weights) if nv is not None and w is not None]\n    denom = sum(w for _, w in pairs)\n    cached_values[addr] = (sum(nv * w for nv, w in pairs) / denom) if denom else None\n\n# Save with formulas\nwb.save(OUTPUT_PATH)\n\n# -----------------\n# Step 5: Inject cached values into Task sheet XML\n# -----------------\n# Modify the saved xlsx in-place by rewriting it via a new zip.\n\ntmp_out = OUTPUT_PATH.with_suffix('.tmp.xlsx')\n\nwith zipfile.ZipFile(OUTPUT_PATH, 'r') as zin:\n    task_xml_path = get_task_sheet_xml_path(zin, 'Task')\n    # Read and patch Task sheet XML\n    task_xml_bytes = zin.read(task_xml_path)\n    patched_task_xml = inject_cached_values_into_sheet_xml(task_xml_bytes, cached_values)\n\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(tmp_out, 'w', compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zout:\n        for item in zin.infolist():\n            data = zin.read(item.filename)\n            if item.filename == task_xml_path:\n                data = patched_task_xml\n            zout.writestr(item, data)\n\n# Replace output\nOUTPUT_PATH.unlink()\ntmp_out.replace(OUTPUT_PATH)\n\n# -----------------\n# Step 6: Validate\n# -----------------\nwb_v = openpyxl.load_workbook(OUTPUT_PATH, data_only=True)\nws_v = wb_v['Task']\nprint('Validation (data_only=True):')\nfor addr in ['H12','I12','H35','H42','H50','L50']:\n    print(addr, ws_v[addr].value)\n\nwb_f = openpyxl.load_workbook(OUTPUT_PATH, data_only=False)\nws_f = wb_f['Task']\nprint('\\nValidation (data_only=False formulas):')\nfor addr in ['H12','H35','H42','H50']:\n    print(addr, ws_f[addr].value)\n\nprint('\\nWrote:', OUTPUT_PATH)\nPY","description":"Populate formulas, compute numeric results, inject cached values, and validate output","timeout":120000,"run_in_background":false,"dangerouslyDisableSandbox":false}}],"model":"openai/gpt-5.2-20251211","stop_reason":null,"stop_details":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":null,"cache_read_input_tokens":null,"cache_creation":null,"inference_geo":null,"server_tool_use":null,"service_tier":null,"speed":"standard"},"provider":"OpenAI","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"07f0e3b4-c7c5-4a4f-8209-105c8df76cc4","uuid":"f97c59d5-3323-49c1-870e-a9e06413966c"}
